Let's develop active-set KKT method in detail.

Given a nonnegative sequence length n. Let M=max c, active lags Ω = {k: c_k ≥ M - ε}. We want find a (scale arbitrary) satisfying stationarity h_j = ∑_{k∈Ω} p_k 2 a_{k-j} = ρ for all j with a_j>0. If we take p uniform, h = 2K a/|Ω|. We can solve K a = vector of length n (maybe all ones) if support full. K_{j,h}=I(j+h∈Ω). K symmetric. Solve K a = 1 via conjugate gradient. Since K nonnegative entries, may be indefinite? Need check positive definiteness. K is Gram matrix? K_{j,h}=1 if j+h in Ω. This is partial sum of Hankel. For Ω={n-1} (center), K_{j,h}=1 if j+h=n-1: anti-identity matrix, symmetric with eigenvalues ±1, indefinite. For Ω interval, K may be positive semidefinite? It is a submatrix of a Toeplitz? Maybe not always PD. We can add regularization: solve (I+τ K) a = 1? Or use least squares. At optimum with many active lags, K likely SPD if active set large. If K indefinite, CG fail. Use LSQR/scipy.sparse? Could construct matrix-free CG on sym K. Regularize by adding diagonal ε: minimize ||K a - 1||^2 with Tikhonov. But if λ weights nonuniform, we can formulate as finding a in null of K? Actually KKT with dual λ: h_j = 2 (K_λ a)_j = ρ. If K_λ indefinite, a maybe not in range? But since h is feasible (positive), can solve linear equations with iterative least squares.

Maybe simpler: Use fixed-point iterative solution:
Initialize a uniform.
For given p (weights distribution over lags, p sum1), define h=2K_p a. We want h constant. Multiplicative update a *= mean(h)/h equalizes. This is convergent if K_p positive? It solves K_p a = const? Let's analyze: update a' = a * ρ / h = a * s / (K_p a). This is iterative for finding positive a such that K_p a constant and same scale. It's like nonnegative linear system solver (Sinkhorn/EM). So algorithm: choose p = normalized weight over active lags (perhaps not just uniform). Iterate:

repeat. Then c uniform? At fixed point, h const, stationarity for p. But c may not be equalized; p was based on old a. Let's design a powerful multiplicative line search function.

`multiplicative_search(a, beta_list, alpha, max_inner, time_limit, n_report_every)`:
Inputs: a array nonnegative length n (will normalize). Returns best_a,best_score.
Pseudo:
```
n=len(a)
a=a.astype(float).copy(); a[~finite]=0; a=np.maximum(a,0)
S=a.sum(); if S<eps: a[:] = 1.0/n
a=a/S
best_score=score(a); best_a=a.copy()
alpha_cur=alpha
for beta in beta_list:
  for outer in range(outer_cycles): # maybe 2
    if time > time_limit: break
    c=fast_conv(a); M=c.max()
    if M<=0: break
    scaled=beta*(c-M)
    p=np.exp(scaled)/exp.sum()
    # mix with p_unif? p = (1-eps)*p + eps/(2n-1)
    p = p*(1-mix) + mix/(2n-1)
    p /= p.sum()
    # maybe clip p min
    inner_improve=False
    for it in range(max_inner):
       if time > time_limit: break
       h = compute_h(a,p)  # 2 cross
       # Some h may be zero if a zeros and p no overlap; floor
       mean_h = h.mean()*n / n? just h.mean()
       h_safe=np.maximum(h, 1e-10*mean_h)
       ratio=mean_h/h_safe
       # line search step
       tried=False
       for eta in eta_candidates (e.g. [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625]):
           a_try=a * np.exp(eta * np.log(ratio)) # ratio^eta
           a_try=np.clip(a_try, floor, 1000)
           S=a_try.sum(); if S<=0: continue
           a_try/=S
           sc=score(a_try)
           if sc < best_score - tol:
               a=a_try; best_score=sc; best_a=a.copy(); inner_improve=True; tried=True; break
           elif sc < a_score_current*1.000? maybe accept if better than current
       if not tried:
           # no improvement, reduce beta/outer break
           break
       # optionally compute current score for line search? We have previous score. Need maintain current_score=score(a)
```
But line search computing score for each candidate means multiple FFT convs. Each candidate c. h only one. If 5 candidates * 200 iters, expensive but maybe okay. Simpler: fixed eta=alpha (no line search), compute score every few iterations. But might diverge. Could use Berkes: update by a *= ratio, renormalize; if score worsens, revert and halve alpha. ```
current_score=best_score

  h=...
  ratio=mean/max(h,eps)
  a_next=a*ratio^eta
  normalize
  sc=score(a_next)
  if sc < current_score:
     a=a_next; current_score=sc; if sc<best: update
  else:
     eta *= 0.5
     if eta<min: break current beta
```

```
def compute_ch(a,p,M,n):
   A=fft(a,M)
   P=fft(p,M)  # if p same, cache P
   c=irfft(A*A,M)[:2n-1]
   corr=irfft(conj(A)*P,M)[:n]
   return c, 2*corr
```

Define top-level:
```
def softmax_obj(x, beta):
   a = softmax(x)
   n=len(a); M=fft_size_for(n)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,M)[:2*n-1]
   cmax=float(c.max())
   scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
   emax=np.exp(scaled)
   sumexp=float(emax.sum())
   L=cmax+np.log(sumexp)/beta
   score=2.0*n*L
   if beta <= 1: maybe
   P=np.fft.rfft(emax/sumexp,M)
   corr=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*P,M)[:n]
   g_a=2.0*corr
   mean=float(np.dot(a,g_a))
   grad=a*(g_a-mean)
   # grad might have tiny nan; return
   return score, grad
```
Because a sum=1 exactly unless numerical. For high beta, P one-hot; h may be zero for some j. grad can have mean correction; okay.

Resample function:
```
def resample_sequence(a, n):
    m=len(a)
    if m==n: return a.copy()
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,m)
    x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    b=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    b=np.maximum(b,0)
    s=b.sum()
    if s>0: b/=s
    else: b.fill(1.0/n)
    return b
```
If we want sum preserved, yes.

But linear interpolation of a sequence with asym orientation? It maps endpoints; if parent not cyclic, okay. Could also upsample by inserting copies? Interpolate smoother.

```

def run_multiplicative_beta(a, best_a, best_score, beta, max_inner, deadline, mix=0.0, alpha_init=1.0, report_every=10):
    n=len(a); M=fft_size_for(n)
    a=a.copy(); normalize
    current_score=best_score if best? maybe compute.
    c=compute_c(a,M)
    Mv=c.max()
    # build p
    p=softmax_beta(c,beta,mix,total=2*n-1)
    P=fft_rfft(p,M)
    alpha=alpha_init
    for it in range(max_inner):
       if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       A=fft_rfft(a,M)
       c=irfft(A*A,M)[:2*n-1]
       corr=irfft(conj(A)*P,M)[:n]
       h=2*corr
       # update best if score improved cheap from c
       sc=2*n*c.max()
       if sc<best_score: best_score=sc; best_a=a.copy()
       current_score=sc
       # h mean
       mean_h=float(h.mean())
       if mean_h <=0: break
       h_safe=np.maximum(h, 1e-12*mean_h)
       log_ratio=np.log(mean_h/h_safe)
       # cap
       cap=1.0 maybe? If alpha large, update large. Use cap = -? We'll cap log_ratio to ±1.0?
       log_ratio=np.clip(log_ratio, -1.0, 1.0)
       # damped factor factor = exp(alpha*log_ratio) capped maybe
       factor=np.exp(alpha*log_ratio)
       a_next=a*factor
       a_next=np.maximum(a_next, 0.0)
       s=a_next.sum()
       if s<1e-12: break
       a_next/=s
       sc_next=score_from_a_next? Need evaluate. We can compute c_next? We already have A for old a; after update need new A for next score. To line search, we can compute sc_next using compute_c(a_next) (one FFT). That's an extra FFT per iter.        A_next=fft_rfft(a_next,M)
       c_next=irfft(A_next*A_next,M)[:2*n-1]
       sc_next=2*n*c_next.max()

```

Implement:
```
def build_p_from_c(c,beta,floor_frac=1e-3,mix_uniform=0.0):
    cmax=c.max(); scaled=beta*(c-cmax); e=np.exp(scaled); emax=e.max()
    e=np.maximum(e, floor_frac*emax)
    e/=e.sum()
    if mix_uniform>0:
       e = (1-mix_uniform)*e + mix_uniform/(len(e))
    return e
```

Let's design `run_lbfgs_softmax(z0, betas, maxfun_per_beta, deadline)` top-level.

```
def run_lbfgs_softmax(z, betas, maxfun, deadline):
   n=len(z); best_score=inf; best_a=None
   for beta in betas:
      if time>deadline: break
      # optional: multiply z by? no
      options={'maxiter':max(100,maxfun//10), 'maxfun':maxfun, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-12, 'maxls':30}
      res=minimize(lse_softmax_obj, z, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options=options)
      a=softmax(res.x)
      sc=score_normalized(a)
      update
      z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-15))
   return best_a,best_score
```
Need choose maxfun based on remaining time. If n=8192, each eval maybe 0.03s. We can set maxfun = int(min(20000, remaining*30)). But if too high, one beta may eat time. We can budget per beta. Maybe allocate 5000 evals for n=8192, 3000 for 16384.

Let's specify helpers in detail.

Utility:
```
def ensure_float_array(x):
    a=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    return a
```
No lambdas.

`norm_array(a)`:
```
a=ensure_float_array(a)
if a.size==0: return np.ones(1)
s=a.sum()
if s<=1e-300: a.fill(1.0/a.size)
else: a/=s
return a
```

`next_pow2(m)`: while p<m: p<<=1. But m can be 1.

`fft_plan_size(n)`:
```
m=3*n-2
p=1
while p<m: p<<=1
return max(p, 8)
```
For n small.

`convolution_fft(a, M=None)`:
```
n=a.size
if M is None: M=next_pow2(2*n-1)
A=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
return np.fft.irfft(A*A,M)[:2*n-1]
```

`score_norm_from_c(c,n)`:
```
return 2.0*float(n)*float(np.max(c))
```
General score:
```
def score_general(a):
   a=ensure_float_array(a); n=a.size; s=a.sum()
   if s<1e-12: return np.inf
   c=convolution_fft(a)
   return 2.0*n*float(np.max(c))/(s*s)
```
But for normalized a, score=2n max.

`compute_c_and_h(a,p,M)`:
```
A=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,M)[:2*a.size-1]
P=np.fft.rfft(p,M)
corr=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*P,M)[:a.size]
h=2.0*corr
return c,h
```
This recomputes P each call; in multiplicative loop, cache P. So another function:
```
def compute_c_h_with_A(A,a_len,p,M) # not needed
```

Implement `run_multiplicative_beta(a, best_a, best_score, beta, top_frac, floor_frac, max_inner, deadline, alpha_init, cap_log)`:
Inputs: a normalized length n. Returns a,best,bestscore.
Pseudo with details:
```
n=len(a); M=fft_plan_size(n)
a=norm_array(a)
current_score=2*n*np.max(convolution_fft(a,M))
best_score=min(best_score,current_score); if best... 
c=convolution_fft(a,M)
# build p
p=build_p(c,beta,floor_frac, mix? maybe top)
P=np.fft.rfft(p,M)
alpha=alpha_init
min_alpha=1e-4
no_improve_count=0
for it in range(max_inner):
    if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,M)[:2*n-1]
    corr=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*P,M)[:n]
    h=2.0*corr
    sc=2.0*n*float(np.max(c))
    if sc<best_score:
        best_score=sc; best_a=a.copy()
    current_score=sc
    mean_h=float(np.mean(h))
    if mean_h <= 1e-300: break
    h_safe=np.maximum(h, 1e-10*mean_h)
    log_ratio=np.log(mean_h/h_safe)
    log_ratio=np.clip(log_ratio, -cap_log, cap_log)
    accepted=False
    # Try current alpha, maybe halved if fail
    try_alpha=alpha
    for _ in range(3):
        if try_alpha < min_alpha: break
        factor=np.exp(try_alpha*log_ratio)
        a_try=a*factor
        # floor
        a_try=np.maximum(a_try, 1e-300)
        s=a_try.sum()
        if s <= 1e-12: try_alpha *=0.5; continue
        a_try=s? a_try/=s
        A_try=np.fft.rfft(a_try,M)
        c_try=np.fft.irfft(A_try*A_try,M)[:2*n-1]
        sc_try=2.0*n*float(np.max(c_try))
        if sc_try < current_score:
            a=a_try; A=A_try; c=c_try; current_score=sc_try
            alpha=try_alpha
            accepted=True
            no_improve_count=0
            break
        else:
            try_alpha *=0.5
    if not accepted:
        alpha *=0.5
        no_improve_count +=1
        if no_improve_count > 10 or alpha < min_alpha:
            break
        # continue with old A? The top of loop will recompute A/c/h from old a. That's okay but wasted? We didn't store A? We have A from old. Could use it to avoid recompute? But next iteration top recomputes; fine.
```
At end, return best.

`build_p` modes. Let's implement one flexible:
```
def build_p(c, mode, beta, top_frac, floor_frac):
    c=c.astype(float)
    L=len(c); cmax=c.max()
    if mode==0:
       scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
       e=np.exp(scaled)
       emax=e.max()
       e=np.maximum(e, floor_frac*emax)
       p=e/e.sum()
    elif mode==1:
       # top uniform
       idx=np.argpartition(c, -int(max(1, top_frac*L)))[:int(...)]? top largest
       p=np.zeros(L); p[idx]=1.0; p/=p.sum()
    elif mode==2:
       # power
       p=(c/cmax)**beta? If beta as power.
    else...
    return p
```
Need no lambdas. For top uniform, `np.argpartition` maybe O(n). For mode 1, top_frac e.g. 0.2.

Maybe modes:
0 softmax with floor (beta param)
1 top uniform (top_frac param)
2 exponential power: p=(c/cmax + eps)^power normalized.

In schedule, try modes in sequence? Too much. We can use mode 0 for most. Maybe after mode0 stalls, try mode1 top uniform. Could include in pipeline: for each beta, run mode0; after a few, run mode1/2. But time.

Alternatively, p=soft p with beta and floor enough.

Let's define `run_multiplicative_order(a,best,bestscore, orders, total_budget, deadline)`: orders list of (mode,beta/param, max_iter, fraction_time). For each, allocate time proportion.

We can implement a local perturbation function:
```
def perturb_log(a, sigma, rng):
   z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12))
   z += sigma*rng.standard_normal(n)
   # optionally zero-mean
   z -= np.mean(z)
   b=np.exp(z); b/=b.sum(); return b
```
Then run pipel-like improvement. Random with small sigma maybe find. Use different seeds.

- Upsample to 8192, then refine.

Let's analyze possible final score with multiplicative line search. It is essentially coordinate-free gradient descent on log a using current smooth p. If the objective has a smooth valley, it should converge. But line search on exact max may make small moves when many lags near max. Implement top-level `lbfgs_fun(x, beta)`:
```
a = softmax(x)
n = a.size
M = fft_plan_size(n)
A=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,M)[:2*n-1]
cmax=float(c.max())
scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
e=np.exp(scaled)
Z=e.sum()
L=cmax + np.log(Z)/beta
p=e/Z
P=np.fft.rfft(p,M)
h=2.0*np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*P,M)[:n]
score=2.0*n*L
mean_h=float(np.dot(a,h))
grad=a*(2.0*n*(h-mean_h))
# sanitize
grad[~np.isfinite(grad)]=0
return (score, grad)
```
No tuple args? `minimize` calls `fun(x, *args)`. If fun returns tuple, jac=True expects gradient. It can accept function returning (f,g). Good. But if we pass args=(beta,), function signature `def lbfgs_softmax_fun(x, beta):`. Good.

Need beware `beta` from args; no issue.

  res=minimize(lbfgs_softmax_fun, z, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS', jac=True, options=options)

else return score_norm(a),a
```

Implement `build_p(c, mode, beta, top_frac)`:
```
def build_p(c, mode, beta, top_frac):
    L=len(c); cmax=float(c.max())
    p=np.zeros(L)
    if mode==0:
       scaled=beta*(c-cmax); e=np.exp(scaled); emax=e.max()
       floor=0.002*emax
       e=np.maximum(e,floor); p=e/e.sum()
    elif mode==1:
       k=max(1,int(top_frac*L))
       idx=np.argpartition(c, L-k)[-k:]? Need k largest.
       p[idx]=1.0; p/=p.sum()
    elif mode==2:
       # power
       q=c/cmax
       p=q**max(1.0,beta); p=np.maximum(p,1e-6); p/=p.sum()
    elif mode==3:
       # threshold
       thresh=cmax*(1.0 - min(0.4, 1.0/beta))? Or top_frac.
       # Use top_frac if beta<0 else threshold.
    else...
    return p
```

Let's write helper `softmax_z(z)`:
```
def softmax_z(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,dtype=float).ravel()
    z=np.where(np.isfinite(z),z,0.0)
    z=z-z.max()
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<1e-300: return np.ones_like(z)/z.size
    return e/s
```

```
def run_lbfgs_from_array(a,beta,maxfun,deadline):
   if maxfun<=0: return score_norm(a),a
   z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12)); z-=z.mean()
   options={'maxiter': min(3000, max(maxfun,1)), 'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-12, 'maxls':20}
   try:
       res=minimize(lbfgs_softmax_fun, z, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS', jac=True, options=options)
       a2=softmax_z(res.x)
   except Exception:
       a2=a
   sc=score_norm(a2)
   if not np.isfinite(sc): sc=score_norm(a); a2=a
   return sc,a2
```

def structural_starts(n, rng=None):
   starts=[]
   x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   # uniform
   starts.append(np.ones(n)/n)
   # linear asym: a = (1 + 0.8x).clip
   for alpha in [-0.5,-0.2,0.2,0.5,0.8]:
       a=1.0 + alpha*(2*x-1.0)
       a=np.maximum(a,1e-6)
       starts.append(a/a.sum())
   # exponential trend
   for k in [-2.0,-1.0,1.0,2.0]:
       a=np.exp(k*x); a/=a.sum(); starts.append(a)
   # quadratic shifted
   for c in [-0.4,0.0,0.4]:
       a=1.0 + c*(x-0.5) + 0.5*(x-0.5)**2
       ...
   # random smooth
   if rng: noise lowpass
   return starts
```

Structural starts generation top-level:
```
def structural_starts(n, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    res=[]
    res.append(np.ones(n)/n)
    # linear
    for alpha in (0.2,0.5,0.8,-0.5):
       a=1.0 + alpha*(x-0.5)
       a=np.maximum(a,1e-6); res.append(a/a.sum())
    # exponential
    for k in (-2.0,-1.0,1.0,2.0):
       a=np.exp(k*(x-0.5)); res.append(a/a.sum())
    # quadratic centered
    for c1,c2 in ((0.2,0.5),(-0.2,0.5),(0.0,1.0)):
       a=1.0 + c1*(x-0.5) + c2*(x-0.5)**2
       a=np.maximum(a,1e-6); res.append(a/a.sum())
    # smooth random: lowpass
    for ampl in (0.2,0.5):
       z=np.fft.rfft(np.zeros(n)); # no
       # create random lowpass via np.cumsum? simpler: z = np.fft.rfft(np.empty(n)); keep low freq * amp; noise; inverse exp
       noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
       F=np.fft.rfft(noise)
       m=F.size
       keep=max(1, int(0.05*m))
       F[keep:] *= 0.05
       xnoise=np.fft.irfft(F, n)/np.sqrt(n)
       a=np.exp(amp*xnoise)
       a=np.maximum(a,1e-6); res.append(a/a.sum())
    return res
```
No lambda. Need ensure FFT length n not power2? rfft handles. `np.fft.irfft(F, n)` okay.

Now, let's detail `run_multiplicative_beta` code with adaptive p. Ensure we return a copy of best.

```
def run_multiplicative_beta(base_a, best_a, best_score, mode, param, max_inner, deadline):
    a = normalize_pos(base_a)
    n = a.size
    M = fft_plan_size(n)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, M)[:2*n-1]
    sc = 2.0*n*float(np.max(c))
    if sc < best_score:
        best_score = sc; best_a = a.copy()
    # build p
    p = build_p(c, mode, param)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, M)
    h = 2.0*np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*P, M)[:n]
    alpha = 1.0
    cap_log = 1.0
    min_alpha = 1e-3
    tries_max = 4
    accepted_count=0
    for it in range(max_inner):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        mean_h = float(np.mean(h))
        if mean_h <= 1e-300:
            break
        h_safe = np.maximum(h, 1e-8*mean_h)
        log_ratio = np.log(mean_h / h_safe)
        log_ratio = np.clip(log_ratio, -cap_log, cap_log)
        # If all near zero, break
        if float(np.max(np.abs(log_ratio))) < 1e-8:
            break
        current_score = 2.0*n*float(np.max(c))
        if current_score < best_score:
            best_score=current_score; best_a=a.copy()
        accepted=False
        try_alpha = alpha
        for _ in range(tries_max):
            if try_alpha < min_alpha:
                break
            factor = np.exp(try_alpha * log_ratio)
            a_try = a * factor
            a_try = np.maximum(a_try, 1e-300)
            s = float(a_try.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
                try_alpha *= 0.5
                continue
            a_try /= s
            A_try = np.fft.rfft(a_try, M)
            c_try = np.fft.irfft(A_try*A_try, M)[:2*n-1]
            sc_try = 2.0*n*float(np.max(c_try))
            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < current_score:
                a = a_try
                A = A_try
                c = c_try
                current_score = sc_try
                alpha = try_alpha
                accepted = True
                # update p,h from new c
                p = build_p(c, mode, param)
                P = np.fft.rfft(p, M)
                h = 2.0*np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*P, M)[:n]
                accepted_count += 1
                break
            else:
                try_alpha *= 0.5
        if not accepted:
            alpha *= 0.5
            if alpha < min_alpha:
                break
            # continue trying smaller alpha with same c,h
            continue
        if it > 100 and int(it) % 50 == 0:
            print progress? maybe not too often.
        # if alpha reduces too many no-improve?
    # final best update from current c
    current_score=...
    if current_score<best_score: ...
    return a, best_a, best_score
```
Potential inefficiency: If accepted, we don't break; loop continues until max_inner or deadline. Could get stuck with tiny improvements. But line search stops if no downhill. At convergence, no accepted and alpha small break. Good.
No-improve count: If many no-improve with small alpha, break. Use `stall=0`; if not accepted and alpha <0.1 maybe break. Simpler: if try loop fails, reduce alpha; if alpha<min break. If alpha not min, continues with same c,h; likely will try smaller and accept. If still fail, eventually break. Good.

But if current_score has many near ties, `sc_try < current_score` exact may fail due numerical. Add tolerance: accept if `sc_try < current_score - 1e-15`. If only tiny improvements, may stall; maybe accept if `sc_try < current_score` (strict) okay. Could use `sc_try <= current_score`.

Potential issue: Using candidate A_try and c_try, then update p from c_try. But if c_try has tiny numerical negative max? okay.

After no accept, we continue with same A/c/h and smaller alpha; but at top of next for iteration, we recompute c? No, we don't recompute A/c at top; the loop uses same A/c. Good. If alpha reduced and not accepted, it will try again smaller. This may require evaluating candidate each time; okay.

But if no accept and we continue, the outer `for it` increments; if while alpha not min, it can loop many times evaluating same h with decreasing alpha until min (max tries? Wait inside try loop only tries 4 alphas decreasing from alpha. If all fail, outside alpha halves and loops iteration again, starting try from new alpha (half). This repeats, so up to 12 alpha halvings before min, each tries 4 overlapping. Fine.

We should not rebuild p if no accept. Good.

What about max_inner if accepted many thousands? It will run until deadline. Good.

`build_p` top uniform mode uses argsort each accepted step (O(n log n) ~16k log) maybe okay but less efficient than softmax. Top uniform maybe used less. It may be expensive if adaptive every step. For mode1, argsort each accept; n=8192, 1000 steps -> 80M log maybe okay but could be slow. We can optimize top uniform by using threshold from cmax? But mode1 only small budget. Use argsort fine.

Potential issue: In `build_p`, for mode 0Softmax: Need handle p when cmax zero. If c all zero, return uniform.
```
def build_p(c, mode, param):
    L=len(c)
    if L==0: return np.array([])
    c=c.astype(np.float64)
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0:
        return np.ones(L)/L
    if mode == 0 or mode == 3 or mode == 4:
       floor_frac = 0.005 if mode==0 else (0.0005 if mode==3 else 0.00002)
       scaled = float(param)*(c-cmax)
       # scaled max 0, min maybe -beta*(range). exp safe? if scaled < -745 underflows to 0; then floor applied. Use np.exp with clip lower -700 to avoid all zeros? Floor with max exp 1. If underflow, e=0, floor positive. okay.
       e = np.exp(np.maximum(scaled, -700.0))
       e = np.maximum(e, floor_frac)
       p=e/e.sum()
    elif mode == 1:
       k = int(max(1, min(L, round(param*L))))
       idx = np.argsort(c)[-k:] if k<L else np.arange(L)
       p=np.zeros(L); p[idx]=1.0; p/=p.sum()
    elif mode == 2:
       q = c / cmax
       p = q ** max(1.0, float(param))
       p = np.maximum(p, 1e-8)
       p = p/p.sum()
    else:
       p=np.ones(L)/L
    return p
```
For mode1 param top_frac maybe 0.2. `np.argsort(c)[-k:]` returns sorted last k; okay. For large L, argsort. If param maybe >1? clamp.

Now, implement `run_parameter_search` with time. It uses minimize per seed with small maxiter; no deadline callback but maxiter fixed. Limit seeds if time.
```
def run_parameter_search(n, seeds, deadline):
    best_a=None; best_score=inf
    for seed in seeds:
        if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
        rng=default_rng(seed)
        w0=rng.standard_normal(6)*0.6
        options={'maxiter':300,'fatol':1e-6,'xatol':1e-5,'adaptive':True}
        try: res=minimize(param_score_fun, w0, args=(n,), method='Nelder-Mead', options=options)
        except: continue
        a=param_from_coeffs(res.x,n)
        sc=score_of(a)
        update
    return best_a,best_score
```
No callback, but 300 evals*6 seeds small. If n=2048, okay. If slow, still <10s.

```
def param_from_coeffs(w, n):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    u=np.zeros(n)
    if w.size >=1: u += w[0]
    t=x-0.5
    if w.size >=2: u += w[1]*t
    if w.size >=3: u += w[2]*t*t
    if w.size >=4: u += w[3]*t*t*t
    if w.size >=5: u += w[4]*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*x)
    if w.size >=6: u += w[5]*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*x)
    u=np.clip(u,-10.0,10.0)
    a=np.exp(u)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: a=np.ones(n)/n
    else: a/=s
    return a
```
Use clipping to avoid overflow. If w unbounded Nelder-Mead may push >10; clip. Objective plateau maybe, but okay.

Maybe include a linear trend with `t` not centered? Good.

def process_candidate(a_seq, budget_seconds, deadline, name, final_polish=False):
    a = normalize_pos(a_seq)
    if a.size == 0: a=np.ones(8192); a/=a.size
    n=a.size
    current_best = score_of(a)
    if not np.isfinite(current_best):
        a = np.ones(8192); a/=a.size; current_best=score_of(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=current_best
    print(f"[{name}] start n={n} score={best_score:.9f}")
    if budget_seconds <= 1 or time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return best_a,best_score
    mult_frac = 0.70 if final_polish else 0.62
    lb_frac = 0.25 if final_polish else 0.30
    rest_frac = max(0.0, 1.0-mult_frac-lb_frac)
    # mult1
    seq=build_mult_seq(n, budget_seconds, final_polish)
    mult_budget = budget_seconds*mult_frac
    best_a,best_score = run_multiplicative_schedule(best_a,best_score, seq, mult_budget, deadline)
    # lbfgs
    lb_budget=budget_seconds*lb_frac
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - max(20, lb_budget*0.2):
       lb_betas = build_lb_betas(n, final_polish)
       best_a,best_score = run_lbfgs_schedule(best_a,best_score,lb_betas,lb_budget,deadline,n)
    # rest mult final
    if rest_frac > 0 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 5:
       rest_budget=...
       seq2 = build_rest_seq(n, final)
       best_a,best_score = run_multiplicative_schedule(best_a,best_score, seq2, rest_budget, deadline)
    # final exact score
    final_score=score_of(best_a)
    if final_score < best_score: best_score=final_score
    best_a=normalize_pos(best_a)
    print end
    return best_a,best_score
```

```
def run_multiplicative_schedule(best_a,best_score,seq,budget_seconds,deadline):
   if budget_seconds <=0: return...
   total_frac=0.0
   for _,_,f in seq: total_frac += max(0.0, float(f))
   if total_frac <=0: return...
   t0=time.monotonic()
   cum=0.0
   for mode,param,frac in seq:
      cum += max(0.0,float(frac))
      stage_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + budget_seconds * (cum / total_frac))
      if time.monotonic() >= stage_deadline: continue
      a_cur,b_a,b_s = run_multiplicative_beta(best_a,best_a,best_score, mode,param,1000000, stage_deadline)
      best_a=b_a; best_score=b_s
      # optional print
   return...
```

But note: If `run_multiplicative_beta` starts from best_a and improves, returns best. If it does not, current=initial. Next stage starts from best. Good.

Let's write `normalize_pos` carefully. It should not alter if sum zero. Use `np.asarray` may return view; copy.
```
def normalize_pos(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if arr.size==0:
       arr=np.ones(1)
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
       arr.fill(1.0/arr.size)
       return arr
    arr *= 1.0/s
    return arr
```

`ensure_sequence_for_output`:
```
def make_output_list(a):
   a=normalize_pos(a)
   a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   return [float(x) for x in a]
```

`resample_sequence`:
```
def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a=normalize_pos(a)
    m=a.size
    if m == n: return a.copy()
    if n <=0: return np.ones(1)
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    b=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    s=b.sum(); if s<=0: b.fill(1/n)
    else: b/=s
    return b
```
For m=1, interp returns same constant.

Detailed code:

```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

_LBFGS_DEADLINE = 0.0

def lbfgs_callback(xk):
    return time.monotonic() > _LBFGS_DEADLINE
```

`next_pow2`:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    p=1
    m=int(max(1,m))
    while p < m:
       p <<=1
    return p
```

`fft_plan_size(n)`:
```
def fft_plan_size(n):
   n=int(max(1,n))
   return max(8, next_pow2(3*n - 2))
```
For n=1, 1 ->8.

`convolution_fft(a, M=None)`:
```
n=a.size
if M is None: M=next_pow2(2*n-1)
A=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,M)
return c[:2*n-1]
```
If M<2n-1? no.

`score_of(a)`:
```
a=ensure? Do not modify? We can arr=np.asarray... but to avoid copy? Use original numeric.

n=arr.size
if n==0: return inf
if n < 1000? use direct np.dot? FFT fine.
s=float(np.sum(arr))
if not finite or <=1e-12: return inf
arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr),arr,0.0)
arr = np.maximum(arr,0.0)
c=convolution_fft(arr)
mx=float(np.max(c))
if not finite: return inf
return 2.0*float(n)*mx/(s*s)
```

Let's write:
```
def run_multiplicative_beta(base_a, best_a, best_score, mode, param, max_inner, deadline):
    a=normalize_pos(base_a)
    n=a.size
    M=fft_plan_size(n)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,M)[:2*n-1]
    current_score=2.0*float(n)*float(np.max(c))
    if current_score < best_score:
       best_score=current_score; best_a=a.copy()
    p=build_p(c, mode, param)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,M)
    h=2.0*np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*P,M)[:n]
    alpha=1.0; min_alpha=1e-3; cap_log=1.2
    accepted=0; stall=0
    for it in range(int(max_inner)):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
        mean_h=float(np.mean(h))
        if not np.isfinite(mean_h) or mean_h <= 1e-300: break
        h_safe=np.maximum(h, 1e-8*mean_h)
        log_ratio=np.log(mean_h/h_safe)
        log_ratio=np.clip(log_ratio, -cap_log, cap_log)
        if float(np.max(np.abs(log_ratio))) < 1e-10:
             break
        # current_score from c
        if not np.isfinite(current_score): break
        try_alpha=alpha
        best_try=None; best_try_score=np.inf
        for _ in range(4):
             if try_alpha < min_alpha: break
             factor=np.exp(try_alpha*log_ratio)
             a_try=a*factor
             a_try=np.maximum(a_try,1e-300)
             s=float(a_try.sum())
             if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
                 try_alpha *= 0.5; continue
             a_try /= s
             A_try=np.fft.rfft(a_try,M)
             c_try=np.fft.irfft(A_try*A_try,M)[:2*n-1]
             sc_try=2.0*float(n)*float(np.max(c_try))
             if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < current_score:
                  best_try=a_try; best_try_A=A_try; best_try_c=c_try; best_try_score=sc_try; break
             try_alpha *= 0.5
        if best_try is None:
             alpha *= 0.5
             stall += 1
             if stall > 8 or alpha < min_alpha:
                  break
             continue
        # accept
        a=best_try; A=best_try_A; c=best_try_c; current_score=best_try_score; alpha=alpha? set to accepted alpha? We lost try_alpha accepted. Could store accepted_alpha.
```
Need store accepted alpha. In loop, when found, set `accepted_alpha=try_alpha`. Then after: `alpha=accepted_alpha`.

def estimate_maxfun(deadline, n):
    rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem <= 2.0: return 0
    if n <= 8192: rate=18.0
    else: rate=7.0
    mf=int(max(300, rem*rate))
    return int(min(30000, mf))

`run_lbfgs_from_array`:
```
def run_lbfgs_from_array(a,beta,maxfun,deadline):
    if maxfun <=0: return score_of(a), a
    n=a.size; M=fft_plan_size(n)
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12))
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    global _LBFGS_DEADLINE
    _LBFGS_DEADLINE = deadline
    options={'maxiter': int(min(5000, max(100, maxfun//3))), 'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol':1e-13, 'gtol':1e-12, 'maxls':10, 'disp':False}
    try:
       res=minimize(lbfgs_softmax_fun, z, args=(beta,n,M), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options=options, callback=lbfgs_callback)
       a2=softmax_z(res.x)
    except Exception:
       a2=a
    sc=score_of(a2)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
       return score_of(a), a
    return sc, a2
```
If res.status maybe max iter; still use res.x. Good.

`lbfgs_softmax_fun`:
```
def lbfgs_softmax_fun(x, beta, n, M):
    z = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = softmax_z(z)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,M)[:2*n-1]
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax): return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    scaled=float(beta)*(c-cmax)
    scaled=np.clip(scaled, -700.0, 0.0)
    e=np.exp(scaled)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if s<=0: p=np.ones_like(c)/len(c); L=cmax
    else:
       p=e/s
       L=cmax + np.log(s)/float(beta)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,M)
    h=2.0*np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*P,M)[:n]
    score=2.0*float(n)*float(L)
    mean_h=float(np.dot(a,h))
    g=2.0*float(n)*(h-mean_h)
    grad=a*g
    grad=np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if not np.isfinite(score): score=1e100; grad=np.zeros(n)
    return score, grad
```
Wait objective is LSE L, not max. For beta, L computed from e. If beta=0? We don't pass 0. If beta small okay. For beta large, log(s)/beta may be unstable when s close? s>=1 (max scaled0), so okay. If multiple max, s>1.

Potential issue: We clip scaled to -700; e sum at least1. Good.

`score_of(a2)` inside L-BFGS schedule uses general score with sum1; if res poor maybe high. We only accept if lower. Good.

For arrays x length n, y length L, zero pad to M. DFT X_k=∑_{i=0}^{n-1} x_i e^{-2πik i/M}. Y_k=∑_{j=0}^{L-1} y_j e^{-...j}. ifft(conj(X)Y)_t = (1/M)∑_k (∑_i x_i e^{+2πi k i/M})(∑_j y_j e^{-2πi k j/M}) e^{2πi k t/M} = ∑_{i,j} x_i y_j δ_{i - j + t ≡0 mod M} = ∑_i x_i y_{i+t} if 0≤i+t<L, plus wrap terms if M large enough > n+L-1, no wrap for t=0..L-1? For t=0..n-1, i+t ≤ n+L-2 < M, no wrap. So corr[t]=∑_{i=max(0, -t)}^{min(n-1,L-1-t)} x_i y_{i+t}. Our y=p length 2n-1, t=j, i=h, p_{h+j}. Good. h_j=2 corr[j]. Great.

For convolution `A*A`: ifft(X^2)_k = ∑_{i,j} a_i a_j δ_{i+j≡k} = ∑_i a_i a_{k-i} for 0≤k≤2n-2 if M>2n-2. Good.

```python
"""
Improves the previous step-function coefficient search using:
1) a fast FFT-based multiplicative log-space descent with adaptive active-lag
   weighting and exact line search on the score;
2) continuation in the smooth max (LSE) exponent beta;
3) L-BFGS-B polishing on a softmax parameterization with the exact LSE
   gradient;
4) several globalist entry points: parent resampling, low-dimensional
   parameterized densities, structured=random-smooth starts, coarse-to-fine
   resampling, and stochastic log perturbations.
"""

import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

_LBFGS_DEADLINE = 0.0


def lbfgs_callback(xk):
    return time.monotonic() > _LBFGS_DEADLINE


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def fft_plan_size(n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    return max(8, next_pow2(3 * n - 2))


def normalize_pos(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        arr.fill(1.0 / arr.size)
    else:
        arr *= 1.0 / s
    return arr


def convolution_fft(a, m=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if m is None:
        m = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m)
    return c[:2 * n - 1]


def score_of(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    c = convolution_fft(arr)
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return float("inf")
    return 2.0 * float(n) * mx / (s * s)


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = normalize_pos(a)
    m = a.size
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        b.fill(1.0 / n)
    else:
        b /= s
    return b


def make_output_list(a):
    a = normalize_pos(a)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return [float(x) for x in a]


def build_p(c, mode, param):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L

    if mode == 0 or mode == 3 or mode == 4:
        if mode == 0:
            floor_frac = 0.005
        elif mode == 3:
            floor_frac = 0.0005
        else:
            floor_frac = 0.00002
        beta = float(max(1e-6, param))
        scaled = beta * (c - cmax)
        scaled = np.clip(scaled, -700.0, 0.0)
        e = np.exp(scaled)
        e = np.maximum(e, floor_frac)
        p = e / e.sum()
        return p

    if mode == 1:
        frac = float(param)
        if not np.isfinite(frac) or frac <= 0.0:
            frac = 0.2
        frac = min(1.0, frac)
        k = int(max(1, min(L, round(frac * float(L)))))
        if k >= L:
            return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L
        idx = np.argsort(c)[-k:]
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[idx] = 1.0
        p /= p.sum()
        return p

    if mode == 2:
        q = c / cmax
        power = float(max(1.0, param))
        p = q ** power
        p = np.maximum(p, 1e-8)
        p /= p.sum()
        return p

    return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L


def run_multiplicative_beta(base_a, best_a, best_score, mode, param, max_inner, deadline):
    a = normalize_pos(base_a)
    n = a.size
    m = fft_plan_size(n)

    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m)[:2 * n - 1]
    current_score = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c))
    if current_score < best_score:
        best_score = current_score
        best_a = a.copy()

    p = build_p(c, mode, param)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, m)
    h = 2.0 * np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A) * P, m)[:n]

    alpha = 1.0
    min_alpha = 1e-3
    cap_log = 1.2
    stall = 0

    for _ in range(int(max_inner)):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        mean_h = float(np.mean(h))
        if not np.isfinite(mean_h) or mean_h <= 1e-300:
            break

        h_safe = np.maximum(h, 1e-8 * mean_h)
        log_ratio = np.log(mean_h / h_safe)
        log_ratio = np.clip(log_ratio, -cap_log, cap_log)
        if float(np.max(np.abs(log_ratio))) < 1e-10:
            break

        best_try = None
        best_try_A = None
        best_try_c = None
        best_try_score = float("inf")
        accepted_alpha = alpha
        try_alpha = alpha

        for __ in range(4):
            if try_alpha < min_alpha:
                break
            factor = np.exp(try_alpha * log_ratio)
            a_try = a * factor
            a_try = np.maximum(a_try, 1e-300)
            s = float(a_try.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
                try_alpha *= 0.5
                continue
            a_try /= s

            A_try = np.fft.rfft(a_try, m)
            c_try = np.fft.irfft(A_try * A_try, m)[:2 * n - 1]
            sc_try = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c_try))

            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < current_score:
                best_try = a_try
                best_try_A = A_try
                best_try_c = c_try
                best_try_score = sc_try
                accepted_alpha = try_alpha
                break
            try_alpha *= 0.5

        if best_try is None:
            alpha *= 0.5
            stall += 1
            if stall > 8 or alpha < min_alpha:
                break
            continue

        a = best_try
        A = best_try_A
        c = best_try_c
        current_score = best_try_score
        alpha = accepted_alpha
        stall = 0

        if current_score < best_score:
            best_score = current_score
            best_a = a.copy()

        p = build_p(c, mode, param)
        P = np.fft.rfft(p, m)
        h = 2.0 * np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A) * P, m)[:n]

    final_score = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c))
    if final_score < best_score:
        best_score = final_score
        best_a = a.copy()

    return a, best_a, best_score


def run_multiplicative_schedule(best_a, best_score, seq, budget_seconds, deadline):
    if budget_seconds <= 1.0 or not seq:
        return best_a, best_score

    total_frac = 0.0
    for _, _, f in seq:
        total_frac += max(0.0, float(f))
    if total_frac <= 0.0:
        return best_a, best_score

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    cumulative = 0.0
    for mode, param, frac in seq:
        cumulative += max(0.0, float(frac))
        stage_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + budget_seconds * (cumulative / total_frac))
        if time.monotonic() >= stage_deadline:
            continue
        _, best_a, best_score = run_multiplicative_beta(
            best_a, best_a, best_score, mode, param, 1000000, stage_deadline
        )
        print(f"  mult mode={mode} param={param:.6g} best={best_score:.9f}")

    return best_a, best_score


def build_mult_seq(budget_seconds, final_polish):
    if final_polish:
        return [
            (0, 200, 0.20),
            (3, 600, 0.25),
            (1, 0.30, 0.20),
            (0, 1500, 0.15),
            (3, 3000, 0.20),
        ]
    if budget_seconds > 400.0:
        return [
            (0, 10, 0.05),
            (0, 30, 0.10),
            (0, 100, 0.16),
            (0, 300, 0.18),
            (3, 500, 0.18),
            (1, 0.25, 0.12),
            (3, 1000, 0.12),
            (0, 2500, 0.09),
        ]
    if budget_seconds > 150.0:
        return [
            (0, 20, 0.15),
            (0, 100, 0.25),
            (0, 300, 0.30),
            (3, 800, 0.30),
        ]
    return [
        (0, 30, 0.35),
        (0, 200, 0.40),
        (3, 600, 0.25),
    ]


def build_rest_seq():
    return [
        (0, 500, 0.35),
        (3, 1200, 0.35),
        (1, 0.35, 0.30),
    ]


def softmax_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64) / z.size
    return e / s


def lbfgs_softmax_fun(x, beta, n, m):
    z = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    a = softmax_z(z)

    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m)[:2 * n - 1]
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = float(max(1e-6, beta))
    scaled = beta * (c - cmax)
    scaled = np.clip(scaled, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(scaled)
    es = float(e.sum())
    if es <= 1e-300:
        p = np.ones_like(c) / c.size
        L = cmax
    else:
        p = e / es
        L = cmax + float(np.log(es)) / beta

    P = np.fft.rfft(p, m)
    h = 2.0 * np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A) * P, m)[:n]
    score = 2.0 * float(n) * float(L)

    mean_h = float(np.dot(a, h))
    g_a = 2.0 * float(n) * (h - mean_h)
    grad_z = a * g_a
    grad_z = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_z), grad_z, 0.0)

    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return score, grad_z


def estimate_maxfun(deadline, n):
    rem = max(0.0, float(deadline - time.monotonic()))
    if rem <= 2.0:
        return 0
    if n <= 8192:
        rate = 18.0
    else:
        rate = 7.0
    mf = int(max(300, rem * rate))
    return int(min(30000, mf))


def run_lbfgs_from_array(a, beta, maxfun, deadline):
    if maxfun <= 0:
        return score_of(a), a

    a = normalize_pos(a)
    n = a.size
    m = fft_plan_size(n)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
    z -= float(np.mean(z))

    global _LBFGS_DEADLINE
    _LBFGS_DEADLINE = float(max(time.monotonic() + 1e-9, deadline))

    options = {
        "maxiter": int(min(5000, max(100, maxfun // 3))),
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "ftol": 1e-13,
        "gtol": 1e-12,
        "maxls": 10,
        "disp": False,
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(
            lbfgs_softmax_fun,
            z,
            args=(beta, n, m),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            options=options,
            callback=lbfgs_callback,
        )
        a2 = softmax_z(res.x)
    except Exception:
        a2 = a

    sc2 = score_of(a2)
    if not np.isfinite(sc2):
        return score_of(a), a
    return sc2, a2


def build_lb_betas(n, final_polish):
    if final_polish:
        return [500, 1000, 2000]
    if n > 10000:
        return [50, 150, 400, 800]
    return [30, 100, 300, 800, 1500]


def run_lbfgs_schedule(best_a, best_score, betas, budget_seconds, deadline, n):
    if budget_seconds <= 1.0 or not betas:
        return best_a, best_score

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = float(len(betas))
    for i, beta in enumerate(betas):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        cumulative = (i + 1) / total
        stage_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + budget_seconds * cumulative)
        if time.monotonic() >= stage_deadline:
            continue
        maxfun = estimate_maxfun(stage_deadline, n)
        if maxfun <= 0:
            continue
        a_new, sc_new = run_lbfgs_from_array(best_a, beta, maxfun, stage_deadline)
        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_score:
            best_score = sc_new
            best_a = a_new
        print(f"  lbfgs beta={beta:.0f} best={best_score:.9f}")

    return best_a, best_score


def process_candidate(a_seq, budget_seconds, deadline, name, final_polish=False):
    if a_seq is None:
        a = np.ones(8192, dtype=np.float64)
        a /= a.size
    else:
        a = normalize_pos(a_seq)
        if a.size == 0:
            a = np.ones(8192, dtype=np.float64)
            a /= a.size

    n = a.size
    best_score = score_of(a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        a = np.ones(8192, dtype=np.float64)
        a /= a.size
        n = a.size
        best_score = score_of(a)
    best_a = a.copy()

    print(f"[{name}] start n={n} score={best_score:.9f}")

    budget_seconds = float(max(0.0, budget_seconds))
    if budget_seconds <= 1.0 or time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return best_a, best_score

    proc_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + budget_seconds)

    if final_polish:
        mult_frac = 0.68
        lb_frac = 0.24
    else:
        mult_frac = 0.62
        lb_frac = 0.30
    rest_frac = max(0.0, 1.0 - mult_frac - lb_frac)

    mult_budget = budget_seconds * mult_frac
    seq = build_mult_seq(mult_budget, final_polish)
    best_a, best_score = run_multiplicative_schedule(
        best_a, best_score, seq, mult_budget, proc_deadline
    )

    lb_budget = budget_seconds * lb_frac
    rem = max(0.0, proc_deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 20.0 and lb_budget > 20.0:
        lb_budget = min(lb_budget, rem)
        betas = build_lb_betas(best_a.size, final_polish)
        best_a, best_score = run_lbfgs_schedule(
            best_a, best_score, betas, lb_budget, proc_deadline, best_a.size
        )

    rest_budget = budget_seconds * rest_frac
    rem = max(0.0, proc_deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rest_budget > 20.0 and rem > 20.0:
        rest_budget = min(rest_budget, rem)
        seq2 = build_rest_seq()
        best_a, best_score = run_multiplicative_schedule(
            best_a, best_score, seq2, rest_budget, proc_deadline
        )

    exact = score_of(best_a)
    if np.isfinite(exact) and exact < best_score:
        best_score = exact
    best_a = normalize_pos(best_a)
    print(f"[{name}] done  n={best_a.size} score={best_score:.9f}")
    return best_a, best_score


def perturb_log(a, seed, sigma):
    a = normalize_pos(a)
    n = a.size
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    z += float(sigma) * rng.standard_normal(n)
    z = np.clip(z, -10.0, 10.0)
    return softmax_z(z)


def param_from_coeffs(w, n):
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if w.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    t = x - 0.5
    u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if w.size >= 1:
        u += float(w[0])
    if w.size >= 2:
        u += float(w[1]) * t
    if w.size >= 3:
        u += float(w[2]) * t * t
    if w.size >= 4:
        u += float(w[3]) * t * t * t
    if w.size >= 5:
        u += float(w[4]) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    if w.size >= 6:
        u += float(w[5]) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)

    u = np.clip(u, -10.0, 10.0)
    a = np.exp(u)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    else:
        a /= s
    return a


def param_score_fun(w, n):
    return score_of(param_from_coeffs(w, n))


def run_parameter_search(n, seeds, deadline):
    best_a = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    for seed in seeds:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        w0 = rng.standard_normal(6) * 0.7
        options = {"maxiter": 250, "fatol": 1e-6, "xatol": 1e-5, "adaptive": True}
        try:
            res = minimize(
                param_score_fun,
                w0,
                args=(n,),
                method="Nelder-Mead",
                options=options,
            )
            a = param_from_coeffs(res.x, n)
            sc = score_of(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass
    return best_a, best_score


def structural_starts(n, rng):
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    t = x - 0.5
    out = []

    out.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n)

    for alpha in (0.25, 0.55, 0.90, -0.55):
        a = 1.0 + alpha * t
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-6)
        a /= a.sum()
        out.append(a.astype(np.float64))

    for k in (-2.0, -1.0, 1.0, 2.0):
        a = np.exp(k * t)
        a /= a.sum()
        out.append(a.astype(np.float64))

    for c1, c2 in ((0.25, 0.50), (-0.25, 0.75), (0.00, 1.00)):
        a = 1.0 + c1 * t + c2 * t * t
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-6)
        a /= a.sum()
        out.append(a.astype(np.float64))

    for amp in (0.25, 0.60):
        noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
        F = np.fft.rfft(noise)
        keep = max(1, int(0.05 * F.size))
        if keep + 1 < F.size:
            F[keep:] = 0.05 * F[keep:]
        z = np.fft.irfft(F, n)
        z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        a = np.exp(amp * z)
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-6)
        a /= a.sum()
        out.append(a.astype(np.float64))

    return out


def make_coarse_candidate(source, n_coarse, n_target, budget_seconds, deadline):
    if source is None:
        source = np.ones(8192, dtype=np.float64)
    a = resample_sequence(source, n_coarse)
    a_out, sc_out = process_candidate(a, budget_seconds, deadline, f"coarse-{n_coarse}")
    if not np.isfinite(sc_out):
        a_out = resample_sequence(source, n_target)
    else:
        a_out = resample_sequence(a_out, n_target)
    return a_out, sc_out


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + total_budget - 40.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(42 + int(seed))

    parent = None
    try:
        raw = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if raw is not None:
            p = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
            p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
            if p.size > 0:
                parent = normalize_pos(p)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    overall_a = None
    overall_score = float("inf")
    small_a = None
    small_score = float("inf")

    def_using_parent = parent is not None

    # Quick low-dimensional global search for a smooth starter.
    param_quick_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + 90.0)
    best_param_seq, best_param_score = run_parameter_search(2048, range(10), param_quick_deadline)
    if np.isfinite(best_param_score):
        print(f"[param-search] n=2048 score={best_param_score:.9f}")
        if best_param_score < overall_score:
            overall_score = best_param_score
            overall_a = best_param_seq.copy()

    # Coarse-to-fine starter from parent if present.
    coarse_seq8192 = None
    if parent is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 150.0:
        coarse_seq8192, _ = make_coarse_candidate(parent, 1024, 8192, 70.0, deadline)

    candidates = []

    if parent is not None:
        candidates.append(("parent8192", resample_sequence(parent, 8192), 0.30))
        candidates.append(("parent16384", resample_sequence(parent, 16384), 0.20))
    else:
        candidates.append(("uniform8192", np.ones(8192, dtype=np.float64) / 8192.0, 0.15))

    if best_param_seq is not None:
        candidates.append(("param8192", resample_sequence(best_param_seq, 8192), 0.10))
        candidates.append(("param16384", resample_sequence(best_param_seq, 16384), 0.06))

    structs = structural_starts(8192, rng)
    if structs:
        struct_scores = np.empty(len(structs), dtype=np.float64)
        for i, s in enumerate(structs):
            struct_scores[i] = score_of(s)
        order = np.argsort(struct_scores)
        if struct_scores[int(order[0])] < overall_score:
            overall_a = structs[int(order[0])]
            overall_score = struct_scores[int(order[0])]
        if len(structs) >= 2:
            i1 = int(order[0])
            i2 = int(order[1])
            candidates.append(("struct8192-a", structs[i1], 0.09))
            candidates.append(("struct8192-b", structs[i2], 0.05))
            if parent is None:
                candidates.append(("struct16384", resample_sequence(structs[i1], 16384), 0.08))

    if coarse_seq8192 is not None:
        candidates.append(("coarse8192", coarse_seq8192, 0.08))

    for name, seq, frac in candidates:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 60.0:
            break
        rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
        alloc = min(total_budget * float(frac), rem * 0.70)
        if alloc < 25.0:
            continue
        a_out, sc_out = process_candidate(seq, alloc, deadline, name)
        if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < overall_score:
            overall_score = sc_out
            overall_a = a_out.copy()
        if a_out.size <= 8192 and np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < small_score:
            small_score = sc_out
            small_a = a_out.copy()

    # Stochastic perturbations of the current best.
    pert_specs = [
        (70011, 0.12, 0.05),
        (70012, 0.25, 0.05),
        (70013, 0.50, 0.04),
    ]
    for ps, sigma, frac in pert_specs:
        if overall_a is None:
            break
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 60.0:
            break
        rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
        alloc = min(total_budget * float(frac), rem * 0.70)
        if alloc < 25.0:
            break
        a_p = perturb_log(overall_a, ps, sigma)
        a_out, sc_out = process_candidate(a_p, alloc, deadline, f"perturb-{\sigma:.2f}")
        if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < overall_score:
            overall_score = sc_out
            overall_a = a_out.copy()
        if a_out.size <= 8192 and np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < small_score:
            small_score = sc_out
            small_a = a_out.copy()

    # Final polish on the best sequence.
    if overall_a is None:
        overall_a = np.ones(8192, dtype=np.float64) / 8192.0
        overall_score = score_of(overall_a)
    if small_a is None and overall_a.size <= 8192:
        small_a = overall_a.copy()
        small_score = overall_score

    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    final_alloc = min(total_budget * 0.25, rem * 0.95)
    if final_alloc > 30.0:
        a_out, sc_out = process_candidate(
            overall_a, final_alloc, deadline, "final-polish", final_polish=True
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < overall_score:
            overall_score = sc_out
            overall_a = a_out.copy()
        if a_out.size <= 8192 and np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < small_score:
            small_score = sc_out
            small_a = a_out.copy()

    # Prefer length 8192 if it is very close to the best larger length, to
    # keep final evaluation lightweight.
    if overall_a.size > 8192 and small_a is not None and np.isfinite(small_score):
        if small_score <= overall_score + 0.0005:
            chosen = small_a.copy()
            chosen_score = small_score
        else:
            chosen = overall_a.copy()
            chosen_score = overall_score
    else:
        chosen = overall_a.copy()
        chosen_score = overall_score

    out = make_output_list(chosen)

    ev = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
    if ev is not None and (deadline + 35.0) > time.monotonic():
        try:
            verified = float(ev(out))
            if np.isfinite(verified) and verified < chosen_score:
                chosen_score = verified
            print(f"verified score={verified:.9f} internal best={chosen_score:.9f} len={len(out)}")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"verification failed: {e}")

    return out
```
